UEW Card Archive

The Ides of March '98

15 March 1998
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
(The year: 44 BC. Scene opens to a funeral where 6 men, 2 in robes, and 4 in Italian war garbs, and one lady with brown hair and also in ancient Italian garb stand around a grave with a Tombstone that reads "Here lies Julius Caesar, May he rest in Peace". One of the robed men, the older of the bunch, Mark Antony, stands at the head of the grave as the others stand around, 3 on each side. The lady, Cornelia, places her head on the shoulder of the other man in the robe with black hair and a beard, Brutus. The light begins to dim on all but Mark Antony) Mark Antony: "You all did see that on the Lupercal I thrice presented him a kingly crown, Which he did thrice refuse; was this ambition? Yet Brutus says he was ambitious: And, sure, he is an honorable man. I speak not to disprove what Brutus spoke, But here I am to speak what I do know." (The lights dim on Mark Antony leaving only a black screen as the following is heard in a whisper...) "Beware the Ides of March" ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: :::::::::::::::::::::THE IDES OF MARCH '98:::::::::::::::::::: ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::March 15, 1998:::::::::::::::::7:15 PM Bell Time:::::::: ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: THE IDES OF MARCH '98 (((((((((((((())))))))))))))) (((UNITED STATES))) (((((((((((((())))))))))))))) From the United Center in Chicago, Illinois ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [NO DISQUALIFICATION MATCH] -Dark Vanity vs. Gigantor ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [GRUDGE MATCH- FOR A SHOT AT THE UEW ULTIMATE TAG TEAM TITLES] -Guts & Glory vs. The Lethal Leaders ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [FOR THE UEW TELEVISION CHAMPIONSHIP- CHICAGO STREET FIGHT] -Magnus Colby vs. "Mr. Excitement" Alex Extreme (c) ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [MAIN EVENT] [FOR THE UEW AMERICAN CHAMPIONSHIP- SKYSCRAPER MATCH II] Rules: Participants begin match in a steel cage. The first man to walk out the door of the cage, climb to the top of the cage, then climb a ladder high enough to grab the hanging American title is declared the winner of the match and American champion. -Professor Payne (c) vs. Mannifred Lester ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: THE IDES OF MARCH '98 ************ *ENGLAND* ************ From Wembley Stadium in London, England ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [TAPED FIST MATCH] -"Bruiser" Bob Todd vs. "Dangerous" Doug Milne ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [FOR THE UEW TELEVISION TAG TEAM CHAMPIONSHIPS] -The Explosion (c) vs. The Fairchild Foundation ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [RESPECT MATCH] -Robski vs. Sick Rick ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [MAIN EVENT] Kyle Kamikaze vs. "Top Dog" Rick Styles ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: THE IDES OF MARCH '98 ########### ## ITALY ## ########### From the Colosseum in Rome, Italy ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [FOR A SHOT AT THE UEW TELEVISION CHAMPIONSHIP] -"Cyclone" Steve Manley vs. J'ster ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [TEN MAN ELIMINATION MATCH] CO-CAPTAINS "The Bad Dog" Scott Haggarty & Eric Smart Dr. Seth Killjoy Wildstar The Sonic Kid vs. CO-CAPTAINS The Mercenary & "Ladykiller" Chris Tyler The Angel of Death Iron Spike Nevermind ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: [FOR THE UEW ULTIMATE TAG TEAM CHAMPIONSHIPS] -The Suicidal Stallions (c) vs. The Circle of Death ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: MAIN EVENT [FOR THE UEW ULTIMATE CHAMPIONSHIP- TRIANGLE MATCH] -"Crippler" Ivan Kolinski vs. Jeff "Madfox" Matthews vs. "No Worries" Rob Magnum (c) ^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^ ::::::::::::::::^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^/^^/^/^/^/^/^:::::::::::::::: (1998: After the darkness, the lights return to reveal an upper level view of THE UNITED CENTER in Chicago as the crowd cheers. The ring is in the center with 4 levels of stands surrounding it. High above is a Jumbo-tron which shows this same camera view, and hanging below is a cable, with the American title attached to it. The crowd begins to cheer even louder as "Sirius" plays and the Voice of the Chicago Bulls, Ray Clay, begins to yell over the arena's speakers) Ray Clay: LADIES AND GENTLEMEN, WWWELCOME TO THE UNITED CENTER, HOME OF _YOUR_ CHICAGGGGOOOOO BULLS!! (Loud pop) Ray Clay: AND WELCOME TO _YOUR_ UEW'S YEARLY EXTRAVAGANZA, THE _IDES_ _OF_ _MMMMMAAAAARRRRCCCCCHHHHHHH!!!!!_ (The cameras pan around the arena showing the tens of thousands in attendance, on their feet cheering.) Ray Clay: AND NOW, MAY I INTRODUCE TO YOU, HE IS THE ANNOUNCER FOR THE CHICAGO PORTION OF TONIGHT'S BIG EVENT, HERE IS JOSHHHHH TAAAYLOOORRRRR! (Crowd cheers as a light appears above the ring, which shines the UEW logo into the center throughout the darkness. Josh Taylor, under a spotlight, jogs up the aisle, wearing a navy blue armani suit. He enters the ring, raises his arms in the air and is then handed the microphone by an outside ring attendant) Josh Taylor: HELLO AND WELCOME TO THE FIRST UEW EVENT TO TAKE PLACE FROM NOT ONE, NOT TWO, BUT _THREE_ DIFFERENT LOCATIONS!! (Scene shows Josh Taylor standing in the center of the ring, looking around at the crowd who have started a "U-S-A!!" chant.) (Scene switches to Wembley Stadium as the Jumbo-Tron above the ring rests on a stand with legs that stretch out across the entire arena, shows Josh Taylor from Chicago. The crowd has responded with a "U-K!!" chant.) (Scene switches again to the Colosseum in Rome, where the ring is surrounded by fans on three sides and the Jumbo-Tron is on the fourth side of the arena, above the entrance ramp for the wrestlers. Sure enough, a chant of "I-TAL-Y" has started up there.) (Scene returns to Chicago and Josh Taylor in the ring.) Josh Taylor: AND NOW, PLEASE WELCOME, YOUR HOSTS FOR THE CHICAGO PORTION OF THE IDES OF MARCH, HERE ARE STAN CARTER... (A small cheer is heard with a few fans whistling.) Josh Taylor: AND JOHNNY DIAMOND!!! (A few scattered cheers are heard throughout the loud assortment of boos. Stan Carter steps out from the curtain, wearing a black tuxedo, complete with cumberbund, vest, jacket and red bow-tie. Carter waves to the crowd. Johnny Diamond steps out next wearing gray sweat pants and a "South Park: Cartoons kick ass" T-shirt. Both men look up the aisle, towards the ring. Stan Carter then turns left and walks up the steps to the platform raised up above the back row of the floor section, where there is a long table set-up, with a few commentator set-ups side by side. Johnny Diamond follows him up. Stan Carter shakes the hands of some of the commentators on hand, as Johnny Diamond just shakes his head. Carter is handed a microphone.) Stan Carter: With all the hype, with all the publicity and all the enthusiasm leading towards this one night, we are finally here!! Welcome fans, to the IDES OF MARCH '98!! I'm Stan Carter, along with the elegantly dressed as usual Johnny Diamond and we'll be your hosts for the opening third of the card, here in Chicago, Illinois. Later tonight, Hank Gordon and Butch Bagswell will bring you The Ides of March from England, as later still, we'll be in Italy with Mike Haynes and Johnny's cousin, Kevin Diamond. Speaking of Johnny, you don't seem like someone who's happy to be here. Johnny Diamond: Why should I be happy? I'm highly respected in both England AND Italy, yet here I get booed. How come I get booed, while an old man like you gets cheered? Carter: I didn't know you were highly respected in England and Italy. Diamond: Well, I AM!! But NNNOOOOOO, I get stuck in the cesspool of the United States, Chicago! Like anything good ever came out of Chicago. (Loud boos are heard from the crowd.) Carter: And you wonder why you're not popular here. Chicago happens to have many great qualities...like their sports figures!! (Boos turn to cheers.) Diamond: Like who? Carter: Well, for one, the Chicago Bulls, and MICHAEL JORDAN!! One of the greatest athletes today. Diamond: Oh please, more like the most overrated athlete today. Carter: Overrated? Diamond: Damn right, he couldn't even break a .220 batting average and don't even get me started on his fielding abilities... Carter: Hmm. Well, how about the likes of ALEX EXTREME and MAGNUS COLBY?!?!? (another pop from the crowd) Diamond: Okay, I'll give you that, NOW we're talking talent. Carter: Alex Extreme, member of the New Alliance and Magnus Colby, LEADER of the Circle of Death, both Chicago natives and quite popular here will meet head on in one of the most grueling types of matches to come out of this city. THE CHICAGO STREET FIGHT!! Tell them about it, Johnny. Diamond: The rules are simple. There ain't any. Come dressed however you want, Do whatever you want, pin your opponent wherever you want. Pretty cut and dry, with Extreme's TV title on the line. Carter: These two have wrestled one another on two different occasions, and in both instances, NOTHING was settled. Sure, Alex Extreme pulled off the victory in both occasions, but the question of who was the better man was still left unanswered. Tonight, we may finally get that answer, Johnny. Diamond: Their first match was on the DWO reunion card, where it was two former friends, now rivals, trying to show each other up. Their second meeting was part of the 4 corner match at the Saturday Night Special where it was all about winning the TV title. Alex Extreme walked away with the belt amongst the chaos and run-ins, but many feel that Magnus Colby should have won the TV title that night. Since then, their feud has escalated to the point where the ring won't be able to contain them. And that brings us to the Chicago Street fight, which is virtually a Bunkhouse No DQ pinfalls count anywhere in Chicago match. Carter: And, just to point out, this truly WILL be one on one, as the entire Circle of Death stable are in Italy, while The New Alliance's Suicidal Stallions defend against members of that same stable in Italy, plus NA members face each other as the war between Rick Styles and Kyle Kamikaze comes to a head tonight in England. Diamond: Hey, enough build-up for the other cities, they have their OWN hosts! Let's talk about the matches HERE! Carter: Of course, there's the tag team match 6 months in the making. The Lethal Leaders meet Guts and Glory for a guaranteed shot at the UEW Ultimate tag team titles somewhere down the road. This is the same match with the same stipulations that was originally supposed to take place in Yankee Stadium at "Rumble in the Bronx '97" TWO Pay-Per-Views ago, but due to the political circumstances surrounding it, the match did not take place. Finally, it will tonight. Diamond: I remember Guts and Glory using "Political Circumstances" to weasel their way out of that match, but tonight, they'll have no choice but to wrestle this match, and face the Lethal Leaders. Carter: For the record, the team of Guts and Glory stepped aside and virtually sacrificed a possible title shot that night, so that the fans of the UEW could get the match they most wanted to see, the retirement match between The Leaders and then-champions, the F.B.I. I guess it's obvious who won. Diamond: Yeah right, for the record, Guts and Glory saved themselves a beating AND a loss that night at the hands of Lethal. Tonight, they gotta fight. No way out of it THIS time. Carter: And let's talk about the war that just won't end. The war that has gone through many MANY feds, now makes it's way to the UEW. Diamond: You know, I heard they surveyed [name withheld], former president of the NICW and asked him what the most violent feud to ever go through his league was. He told them it was Shawn Riser against Dark Vanity. Carter: Who's "they"? Diamond: I heard they surveyed [name withheld], president of the IWF/WOW and asked him what the most violent feud to ever go through his league was. He told them it was Shawn Riser vs Dark Vanity. Carter: Who's "they"? Diamond: I heard they surveyed Mike what's-his name, former co-president of the DWO, and asked him what the most violent feud he's ever seen was. He said Shawn Riser vs Dark Vanity. Carter: Who's "th... Diamond: Would you shut up, old man? The war between those two has been intense since day one, and it's taken a lot out of BOTH men. Shawn Riser has just about lost his career due to injuries, and Dark Vanity (Points to his head) has lost SO much more. And now, we know who was pushing BOTH of their buttons and keeping that feud going for so long. As usual, you can blame it on the dame. Sandy Fox was porking the TWO of them. Carter: Shawn Riser has now taken up managing and who does he bring back to the UEW, but the former member of the New Alliance, the seven foot madman known only as GIGANTOR! Diamond: I remember Gigantor's career under Mark R. Rose. Rose was the only man who could control this monster, and only barely. Then, it was at last year's Ides of March that Rose finally lost control of Gigantor and that monster attacked and nearly killed officials, security guards AND fans. Finally, the police had to come in and use electric prods to get this monster out of the arena, locked up and thrown in a padded cell. He spent a lot of time there, too. Nearly an entire year. Carter: It was in that New Orleans Detention center that Shawn Riser found Gigantor, took him out and it seems that Riser has more control over Gigantor then Mark R. Rose, or for that matter, anyone else has EVER had. Tonight, Shawn Riser sends Gigantor after his long-time hated enemy, Dark Vanity, who has more at stake in this match then any other fight he's ever been in. Dark Vanity's pride, manhood and perhaps his sanity is all on the line in this match as he looks for revenge against his ex-fiancee's "other man". Diamond: It should be an interesting match, as the man who spent the year in an institution takes on the man who belongs in an institution.....Gigantor vs Dark Vanity. Carter: And that brings us to the Main Event of Chicago. (Camera shot switches from Carter and Diamond, to the American title hanging from the cable high above the ring) Carter: It was just 4 months ago, at our last Pay-Per-View, HOLIDAY HORROR II, where Mannifred Lester did the unthinkable. He BRUTALLY attacked Professor Payne, by jumping him from behind AFTER he had just wrestled that long grueling Triple Threat match. Lester then proceeded to place Payne into the Cannibal Clutch and kept the move hooked for an ungodly amount of time, snapping cartilage in his back and neck! Such a cowardly, vicious and heartless act...why are you laughing, Johnny? This isn't funny!! Diamond: "BRUTALLY" "The unthinkable" "Heartless act" Spare us the drama, old man. Mannifred Lester did what it took to get the Prof's attention, and DAMN, did he get it in a BIG way!! Tonight, he's got the biggest opportunity of his career to walk out of the United Center with the UEW AMERICAN TITLE!! (Camera zooms across the crowd who all cheer and hold up signs.) Carter: Their match tonight, fans, and our Main Event will see these two go at it in THE most dangerous match in UEW history, the SKYSCRAPER MATCH!! The match takes place in a steel cage, with no rules and only one referee who stands OUTSIDE the cage, but escape isn't enough. Diamond: No, once you escape the cage, and you can only do so through the door since the cage does have a ceiling, you have to then first climb the wall of the cage and make your way on top of the cage's roof. Up there is a ladder and the UEW American title hanging from a cable which you can ONLY reach with the help of that ladder. The danger in this match is unbelievable. The cage is 15 feet high. The ladder is a good 12 feet high. To get that belt and walk out of here American champion, you have to climb a good 25 feet high and hope you don't fall. Carter: Needless to say, the dangers of this match equal the danger the competitors have in facing one another. In fact, it was at Rumble in the Bronx '97 that we had our first Skyscraper match, and the J'ster was believed to have "died" during that match. Diamond: That was the rumor, but apparently he got better. Carter: The truth of the matter is, the J'ster fell from the top of the ladder all the way through the cage's ceiling into the ring, leaving him with major injuries. He was rushed off to the hospital and had not been heard from since, until just recently at the Saturday Night Special. Later tonight, he makes his return to the UEW as part of the Ides of March in Italy. Diamond: Is it just me or does it seem that UEWers who die never stay dead? First Robski died, Then Shawn Riser was rumored to be dead and we ALL saw the J'ster fall to his death. Yet they're ALL wrestling tonight. Carter: So, what's your point? Diamond: What are we, the UEW or the X-men? THEY never stay dead either. Carter: Josh Taylor has the microphone, which means we're about ready to hand the commentary reigns over and start our opening match for the Ides in Chicago. But first, let's take a look at the now-infamous wedding ceremony that took place on the Saturday Night Special between Dark Vanity and Sandy Fox, and see how it led up to our first match. Diamond: Conducted by New York's Mayor Rudolph Giuliani, by the way. >>> UEW FLASHBACK- February 7, 1998- ***SATURDAY NIGHT SPECIAL*** Rudy Giuliani: Glen Osbourne, do you take this woman to be your lawful wedded wife, to have and to hold, to love and to cherish til death do you part? Dark Vanity: I do. (The crowd pops.) Rudy Giuliani: Sandy Fox, do you take this man to be your lawful wedded husband, to honor and to obey, to love and to cherish, til death do you part? Sandy Fox: I...... (Sandy starts crying. The crowd is cheering.) Sandy Fox: I....... (Glen is starting to sweat.) Rudy Giuliani: Shall I repeat the question? Sandy Fox: I ....can't. Dark Vanity: Wha...what? Sandy Fox: Glen, I can't. There's somebody else. Dark Vanity: What are you talking about? Sandy Fox: When you dumped me back in the summer, I met someone else. I think I love him, but I love you too. Dark Vanity: Who? Sandy Fox: That's not important. Let's just please, stop this. Dark Vanity: WHO? Sandy Fox: Ri.... Dark Vanity: Styles? You fell in love with Rick?? Sandy Fox: Not Rick....Riser! Shawn Riser. Dark Vanity: I can't believe this. (Dark Vanity pushes Rudy Giuliani out of the way and leaves toward the back, leaving Sandy crying. The crowd is booing Sandy Fox. Sandy falls to the floor.) <<< Carter: Dark Vanity and Shawn Riser write another chapter in their epic feud, after we take a look at this! ------------------------------ A broken heart has caused many a man to go over the edge. >DARK VANITY< February 7, 1998 was supposed to be the happiest day of Glen Osbourne's life. (photograph of DV and Sandy Fox smiling) He was getting married to the love of his life.... his best friend his soulmate. But what was supposed to be the happiest moment in his life, turned out to be the darkest moment of his life. >>> Rudy Giuliani: Sandy Fox, do you take this man to be your lawful wedded husband, to honor and to obey, to love and to cherish, til death do you part? Sandy Fox: I...... (Sandy starts crying. The crowd is cheering.) Sandy Fox: I....... (Glen is starting to sweat.) Rudy Giuliani: Shall I repeat the question? Sandy Fox: I ....can't. Dark Vanity: Wha...what? Sandy Fox: Glen, I can't. There's somebody else. (FAST FORWARD) Dark Vanity: WHO? Sandy Fox: Not Rick....Riser! Shawn Riser. <<< Glen Osbourne lost his marriage, his heart, his soul, and his fiancee to his arch-rival Shawn Riser. Wasn't it to be expected that he'd lose his mind too? (clip of DV in a straitjacket....laughing) Dark Vanity goes for revenge against Shawn Riser and his new protege Gigantor. He wants vengeance ten-fold against the man who took it all from him. NO DISQUALIFICATION MATCH Dark Vanity vs. Gigantor Ides of March- U.S.A. NEXT!!!! ---------------------------------- Long-time hated enemies settle their NEWEST gripe with one another. Gigantor vs Dark Vanity in Part 2 of the Ides from Chicago. Stan Carter: Imagine if you will, having a bitter HATED enemy for years, both you and he have taken so much away from one another, inflicted such harm that it's practically driven you INSANE!! And the only semblance of normalcy and relief is at home with your loved one. Now imagine, if all of the sudden, that loved one leaves you for that same enemy. That is exactly what Dark Vanity is going through...a situation that could only happen here, in the UEW. Diamond: Thank you, Rod Serling. Carter: I'm only trying to express the hatred Dark Vanity has for Shawn Riser, and how badly he wants to get his hands on him. Diamond: But he's NOT getting his hands on Shawn Riser. Riser retired and went into managing. Tonight, Dark Vanity's going to have to settle for his protege, the FORMER protege of Rick Styles' manager, Mark R. Rose. Of course, I'm talking about GIGANTOR!! And let me just say, this match is Gigantor's type of rules, which means none. Carter: We all know the violent tendencies of Gigantor, but right now, Dark Vanity is no stranger to the world of "No Disqualifications" and right now, he has ALL the cause in the world! Fans, "Pretty Boy" Charlie Underwood and Brad Savage are standing by in the commentator's booth next to our own, ready to start the Ides of March- U.S.A.!! By the way, Johnny, notice THEY both came in tuxedos as well. Diamond: So, what's your point? (Both men look down at Johnny Diamond's t-shirt.) Carter: Take it away, guys. (Camera switches to show two men at ringside. Both are wearing tuxedos...) Brad Savage: Good evening ladies and gentlemen! And welcome to UEW's Mega-Event, Ides of March! I'm Brad Savage and with me here is "Prettyboy" Charlie Underwood. "Prettyboy" Charlie Underwood: You're damn right that this is the Mega-Event, they got _me_ to broadcast one of the matches! That in itself makes it an Event, much less a Mega one. BS: You majored in Egoism in college didn't you? PCU: Someone had to. BS: Anyhow, we're set to bring to you the No Disqualification match between Dark Vanity and Gigantor. This one's really heated up in the past weeks, huh? PCU: That it has. Dark Vanity's lost his marbles since his wife-to-be left him at the alter and joined up with Shawn Riser and Gigantor. BS: That said, let's take it to the ring... Josh Taylor: The following contest is set for one fall and is a No Disqualification Rules match. Introducing first, standing at 6 foot 10, weighing in at 365 pounds, from parts unknown.........here is...... GIIIIIIIIIGAAAAAAANNNNNTTTTTOOOORRRRRRRR!!!!!! ("This Love" by Pantera roars through the arena as the almost seven foot giant steps from behind the curtain...) PCU: Check it out, Gigantor's brought some back up with him in the form of Shawn Riser. BS: Did you expect any less? Riser IS his manager. PCU: A 6'8" 285 pound manager. BS: Gigantor looks about as focused as he can be for this match. Who are you putting your money on, Charlie? PCU: I'm gonna have to go with Gigantor, he's almost seven feet tall and has Riser at ringside with him. Plus Riser's got the distraction of Sandy Fox hanging over DV's head. JT: And his opponent, standing at 6 foot 3, weighing in at 220 pounds, he hails from UTICA, NEW YORK.....here is...... DAAAAAARRRRRKK VAAAAAAAANNNIIIITTTTYYYYYY!!!!!! BS: DARK VANITY EXPLODES FROM BEHIND THE CURTAIN!!!! He's got that Singapore Cane of his as well as a fire extinguisher!!!! PCU: DV marches right up the aisle and to the ring! Instead of entering the ring, he goes right after Riser with those weapons! Now, he's chasing Riser around the ring!!! Riser heads towards the back! DV proceeds to make his way into the ring. Gigantor charges in.... BS: And DV hoses him down with that fire extinguisher!!! Gigantor's got foam all in his eyes! He can't see where he is!!!! PCU: DV runs into the ropes and rams that fire extinguisher into the head of Gigantor. AND DV'S POUNDING THE CRAP OUTTA GIGANTOR!!! BS: DV's pummeling Gigantor with left and rights against the ropes!!!! The ref can't break it up either!!! DV's going crazy and Gigantor can't see!!! PCU: Waitaminute!! Shawn Riser runs up the aisle, jumps onto the apron and goes right to the top rope! Double axehandle to the back of DV and that finally breaks the two combatants up. Riser rolls to the outside and gives the fans a double one-finger salute. BS: Gigantor's finally got that foam out of his eyes. Gigantor's got DV by the throat.... AND PICKS HIM UP OVER HIS HEAD!!!! PCU: DV's being choked out! His feet are dangling below him! Look at his face! It's absolutely red!!! BS: DV's kicking and kicking.... And nails Gigantor in the lower regions! That broke the hold! PCU: OOOOOHHHHH! Gigantor will be feeling that one for a while. Gigantor falls to his knees and DV catches a quick breath. DV reaches down and grabs his Cane. HE SMACKS GIGANTOR ACROSS THE HEAD WITH IT! DV off the ropes and lands a flying clothesline onto the prone Gigantor! BS: Gigantor's down, and DV runs in with the cover.... 1 . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 2 . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . And Gigantor presses DV halfway across the ring!! PCU: DV's back up to his feet, picks up the cane and cracks the it over Gigantor's head. Gigantor just stares at him. DV with the cane again! No effect!! BS: DV runs off the ropes........and is caught by Gigantor in a bearhug!!! Gigantor rams DV into the corner.... AND AGAIN!!! PCU: DV's dropped his cane. Now Riser runs over and snatches it. What's he doing with it? He's throwing it into the crowd! Some lucky fan now has an authentic UEW Singapore Cane! BS: Hold on, some crazy fan just grabbed Riser and is choking him with fishing wire!!!! PCU: Meanwhile in the ring, Gigantor's dismantling Dark Vanity! Uh oh, Gigantor just caught a glimpse of Riser being attacked! Gigantor slides out of the ring and grabs the attacker by the head!! BS: Gigantor just grabbed the wig off that attacker! SCREWED OUT OF YET ANOTHER TITLE IN UEW!!!! WAH WAH WAH!!!! Mike Haynes- LOOK AT THE EXPRESSION ON IVAN'S FACE!!!! HE CAN'T BELIEVE THAT THIS IS HAPPENING TO HIM ALL OVER AGAIN!!!! Kevin Diamond- UEW Officials _2_, Ivan Kolinski 0. Do I see a tear?? IS IVAN KOLINSKI CRYING!?!?!? Mike Haynes- HE IS UPSET BECAUSE HE WAS SCREWED OUT OF A TITLE IN UEW AGAIN!!!! HE'S GOING OVER TO TALK TO THE REFEREE!!!! Kevin Diamond- DON'T CRY TO THE REFEREE!!! HE AIN'T GONNA HELP YA!!! Mike Haynes- Meanwhile, Rob Magnum is at the top of the ramp....the official is walking over to hand him the UEW Ultimate title which is still in his possession. LOOK AT HIM LAUGH AT IVAN KOLINSKI!!! HE SEES THE AGONY IN IVAN KOLINSKI'S EYES AND HE IS LOVING THIS!!!! WHAT A HORRIBLE HUMAN BEING!!! Kevin Diamond- But he's still the champ. Ivan should look up to him as a role model. Rob's never been screwed like this!!!! Mike Haynes- Ivan Kolinski is pleading his case to the officials. If President Jay was here, we'd be able to get an official ruling on the situation. Kevin Diamond- But instead, he was over in England kissing Robski's ass. Mike Haynes- My heart breaks for Ivan......I don't think there's anything the officials can do in this situation. Kevin Diamond- Look at Rob Magnum!!! He is getting a HUGE kick out of this whole thing!!!! Mike Haynes- Poor Ivan.....that's really all I have to say. He was so close to the title. Poor Ivan. Kevin Diamond- You get paid for this???!? Mike Haynes- He's still talking it over with the officials....trying to get them to see his side. Kevin Diamond- Hopefully he's trying to get them to see the side with less blubber on it. Mike Haynes- It looks like there's nothing else Ivan could say to the officials. I feel so terrible for him. Kevin Diamond- Well at least feel good for Rob!! He's still got the title!!! Mike Haynes- The only thing I feel for Rob Magnum is pity. The fact that he could nearly end Jeff Matthews' life during one of his temper tantrums, and then sit here and laugh at Ivan Kolinski's pain makes me sick to my stomach. Once again, UEW has screwed Ivan Kolinski. Kevin Diamond- Oh yeah!? BRET screwed Bret....wait, wrong show. Mike Haynes- Rick "The Ring" Announcer has the microphone again. He's probably going to tell the UEW fans that haven't left already that the show is over and they should go home to their families. Kevin Diamond- Or back to the restaurants....I'm starving. Mike Haynes- Rick wants our attention..... Rick "The Ring" Announcer- LADIES AND GENTLEMEN........ Kevin Diamond- Ladies and gentlemen, there is nothing to see here. Except for a fat whale in the ring crying because he couldn't win the title...... Mike Haynes- Be quiet! Rick "The Ring" Announcer- UPON FURTHER DELIBERATION........THE UEW OFFICIALS HAVE DECIDED..... Kevin Diamond- To give Ivan Kolinski an appointment for a liposuction....on the house!!! Rick "The Ring" Announcer- THAT THE TRIANGLE MATCH ___CANNOT___ END ON A DISQUALIFICATION...... Mike Haynes- What? Kevin Diamond- WHAT? Rick "The Ring" Announcer- THEREFORE......ROB MAGNUM ____MUST____ DEFEND THE UEW ULTIMATE CHAMPIONSHIP AGAINST "CRIPPLER" IVAN KOLINSKI AS SOON AS POSSIBLE!!!! Kevin Diamond- The next SUNDAY NIGHT HEAT is not until the first week in April. Rob Magnum will at least have some time to prepare and let his knee recover a bit. Mike Haynes- Hold it..... Rick "The Ring" Announcer- WHICH WILL BE TONIGHT!!!! ROB MAGNUM HAS THIRTY SECONDS TO RETURN TO THE RING OR ELSE FORFEIT THE TITLE TO IVAN KOLINSKI!!!!!! (THE CROWD EXPLODES!!!!!!!!!) Kevin Diamond- WHAT?!?!?! WHAT KIND OF CRAP IS THIS?!!?!?!?! Mike Haynes- ROB MAGNUM CAN'T BELIEVE IT EITHER!!!!!! HE IS SCREAMING FROM THE OTHER SIDE OF THE RAMP!!!! Kevin Diamond- HE DOESN'T HAVE TO DO THIS!!!! HE'S DEFENDED HIS TITLE ALREADY!!!! Mike Haynes- HE DOES HAVE TO DO THIS!!! LISTEN TO THE CROWD COUNT DOWN MAGNUM!!!! Crowd: TWENTY-SEVEN!!!! TWENTY-SIX!!!!....... Kevin Diamond- MAGNUM IS PANICKING!!! HE DOESN'T WANT TO FACE IVAN KOLINSKI IN THE RING FOR THE TITLE!!!! Crowd: TWENTY-FOUR!!!! TWENTY-THREE!!!!! Mike Haynes- ROB MAGNUM HAS TWENTY ONE SECONDS TO DECIDE WHETHER WE WANTS TO DEFEND THE TITLE AGAINST IVAN KOLINSKI OR FORFEIT IT!!!! Crowd: NINETEEN!!!! EIGHTEEN!!!! SEVENTEEN!!!! Kevin Diamond- HE DOESN'T WANT TO GO DOWN THE RAMP!!! BUT HE DOESN'T WANT TO FORFEIT THE TITLE EITHER!!!! Crowd: FOURTEEN!!!! THIRTEEN!!!!! Mike Haynes- IVAN KOLINSKI IS WAVING MAGNUM ON DOWN!!! HE WANTS TO FINISH THIS MATCH ONCE AND FOR ALL!!!! Crowd: ELEVEN!!!!! TEN!!!!!!! NINE!!!!! Mike Haynes- ROB MAGNUM IS SPRINTING TOWARDS THE RING, BAD WHEEL AND ALL!!! LEFT BY KOLINSKI!!!! LEFT BY KOLINSKI!!!! LEFT BY KOLINSKI!!!!!! KOLINSKI HAS MAGNUM REELING!!!! Kevin Diamond- MAGNUM WITH A THUMB TO KOLINSKI'S EYE! Mike Haynes- MAGNUM RUNS AND SLAMS KOLINSKI'S EYE INTO THE EXPOSED TURNBUCKLE!!!! Kevin Diamond- BRILLIANT STRATEGY BY ROB MAGNUM!!! HE'S GOING AFTER THE INJURED EYE THAT KEPT KOLINSKI OUT OF ACTION FOR THE LAST HALF OF 1997!!!!! Mike Haynes- Magnum drags Kolinski to the center of the ring...HE BENDS KOLINSKI'S HEAD DOWN!!!! Kevin Diamond- IT'S SOUTHERN COMFORT TIME AGAIN!!!!!!!!!!!!! Mike Haynes- ROB MAGNUM PICKS HIM UP!!!!!!!!! THE KNEE BUCKLED UNDER HIM!!!! KOLINSKI LANDS ON HIS FEET!!!!! Kevin Diamond- MAGNUM'S INJURED KNEE WOULDN'T LET HIM GET THE 313 POUND KOLINSKI ALL THE WAY IN THE AIR!!!! Mike Haynes- KOLINSKI LOOKS OUT OF ONE EYE......HE SCOOPS UP THE 360 POUND ROB MAGNUM!!!!!! Kevin Diamond- NO!!!!!! NO!!!! NO!!!!!!! Mike Haynes- ______THE______ _______COLD_______ _______WAR_______!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! Kevin Diamond- NO!!! ROB, NO!!!!! Mike Haynes- KOLINSKI GOT ALL OF IT!!!!! ONE!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! . . . . . . . . TWO!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! . . . . . . . . THREEEEEEEE!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! Kevin Diamond- NO!!!! Mike Haynes- YES!!!!! YES!!!!! YES!!!!!! YES!!!!! Kevin Diamond- NO!!!! NO!!!!! Mike Haynes- WE HAVE A ___NEW___ UEW ULTIMATE CHAMPION AND HE'S "THE CRIPPLER" IVAN KOLINSKI!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! Mike Haynes- LADIES AND GENTLEMEN, WE ARE OUT OF TIME....FOR THE ENTIRE UEW BROADCAST TEAM FROM CHICAGO TO LONDON AND FINALLY HERE IN ROME....FOR KEVIN DIAMOND, I AM MIKE HAYNES.....SO LONG EVERYBODY FROM THE IDES OF MARCH '98!!!!!
